# Backend security audit — DigitalMyHotels FastAPI

Read-only review of `backend/app/api/v1/` and `backend/app/services/`. Focus: tenant isolation, then money, subscription, uploads, auth, races.

---

### 1. HIGH — Refund max includes deposit but only reduces advance

**File:** `backend/app/services/payments.py` — `refund_payment` ~L416–460

`max_refundable = advance_amount + security_deposit`, but only `booking.advance_amount` is reduced (floored at 0). A refund larger than advance leaves `security_deposit` unchanged while cash leaves the hotel.

**Fix:** Allocate refund against advance first, then deposit (or split explicitly), then `settle_booking_amounts`.

---

### 2. HIGH — Attendance `selfie_key` not hotel-scoped on write

**File:** `backend/app/services/attendance.py` — `_apply_check_in` ~L210–211; `self_check_in` ~L306; serve path ~L1097–1099; upload ~L1337–1338

Upload keys are under `hotels/{hotel_id}/…`, but check-in stores whatever `selfie_key` the client sends. Serving uses `get_bytes(rec.check_in_selfie_key)` with no prefix check. API returns the key (`staff.py` selfie upload). A leaked/guessed key from hotel B can be attached to hotel A’s record and later read by A’s managers.

**Fix:** Require `selfie_key.startswith(f"hotels/{hotel_id}/")` (and preferably `…/staff/selfies/{user_id}/`) before save; reject otherwise.

---

### 3. HIGH — Booking/stay totals omit GST; invoices recompute with GST

**Files:**

- `backend/app/services/bookings.py` — `create_booking` ~L216 (`total = subtotal - discount`, no GST)
- `backend/app/services/invoices.py` — `generate_invoice` ~L110–119 (`calculate_gst` on room lines)
- `backend/app/services/stay.py` — `quote_checkout` / `compute_settlement` ~L741–820 / ~L853–907 (GST-aware)

Desk booking totals and “due” can disagree with checkout quote and invoice for the same stay.

**Fix:** One pricing function (room + charges + GST − discount) used by create, update, checkout, and invoice.

---

### 4. HIGH — `reverse_checkout` does not reverse money side-effects

**File:** `backend/app/services/stay.py` — `reverse_checkout` ~L1181–1247

Reopens booking/rooms and marks checkout reversed; does not reverse checkout-time payment, late-fee ledger debit, invoice, or void charges added at checkout.

**Fix:** On reverse: void/refund checkout collection, reverse late-fee ledger, cancel/void invoice, or hard-block reverse once money/invoice exists.

---

### 5. HIGH — Expired subscription still allows many mutations

**File:** `backend/app/services/subscriptions.py` — `assert_transactions_allowed` ~L47–70

**Calls it:** `bookings.create_booking`; `stay` check-in / transfer / check-out; `charges.add_charge`; `payments` collect/correct/refund; `invoices.generate_invoice`; `expenses.create_expense`.

**Does not call it (examples):** `bookings.update_booking` / `cancel_booking` / room replace-add; `guests` create/update/docs; `rooms` / room-types; `staff` / `team`; `attendance` check-in/out/leave; `ops` closing/handover; `housekeeping`; `expenses.create_recurring` / approve-reject; hotel settings / UPI / gallery.

Expired hotels can still cancel/edit stays, manage staff, mark attendance, close day, etc.

**Fix:** Central middleware/dependency on all mutating hotel routes, or call assert in every mutating service entrypoint; document intentional read-only exceptions.

---

### 6. MEDIUM — Housekeeping loads Room/Booking by primary key only

**File:** `backend/app/services/housekeeping.py` — `ensure_task_for_room` ~L67–86

`select(Room).where(Room.id == room_id)` and `select(Booking).where(Booking.id == booking_id)` omit `hotel_id`. Callers today pass scoped IDs, but a mismatched `room_id` would create a task under hotel A while reading hotel B’s `room_number` / guest name into notifications.

**Fix:** Always filter `Room.hotel_id == hotel_id` and `Booking.hotel_id == hotel_id` (same as `_room`).

---

### 7. MEDIUM — Notification mark-read loads by ID, scopes in Python

**File:** `backend/app/services/notifications.py` — `mark_read` ~L161–173

`Notification.id == notification_id` only; hotel/user checks after. Rows with `hotel_id IS NULL` and non-matching/`NULL` `user_id` can be readable/markable across tenants.

**Fix:** Query with `(hotel_id == tenant.hotel_id OR user_id == tenant.user_id)` and never return another hotel’s row.

---

### 8. MEDIUM — Guest documents: download auth OK; query missing `hotel_id`

**Files:** `backend/app/services/guests.py` — `get_document_bytes` ~L90–105; `list_documents` ~L82–85; `backend/app/api/v1/guests.py` ~L178–210

Serving goes through hotel-scoped `get_guest` — good. Document select uses `guest_id` only (no `GuestDocument.hotel_id`).

**Fix:** Add `GuestDocument.hotel_id == hotel_id` on list/get for defense in depth.

---

### 9. MEDIUM — Room re-fetch by ID after lock (bookings)

**File:** `backend/app/services/bookings.py` — `replace_booking_room` ~L594–599; `add_room_to_booking` ~L728–734

`_lock_rooms(..., hotel_id, …)` is correct; later `Room.id == …` reload omits `hotel_id`. Not exploitable if lock always runs first; fragile if refactored.

**Fix:** Include `Room.hotel_id == hotel_id` on every room load.

---

### 10. MEDIUM — Checkout quote vs settlement can diverge mid-flow

**File:** `backend/app/services/stay.py` — `check_out` ~L924–1008; `quote_checkout` vs `compute_settlement`

Quote includes proposed charges + effective discount; settlement uses DB charges + `booking.discount_amount`. Order (write discount → add charges → settle) usually matches, but any failure between steps or second `compute_settlement` after payment can drift.

**Fix:** Persist quote snapshot on checkout row and settle from that snapshot only.

---

### 11. MEDIUM — Password change does not revoke refresh sessions

**File:** `backend/app/services/auth.py` — `change_password` ~L255–270 vs `confirm_password_reset` ~L242–244

Reset revokes all refresh tokens; change-password does not. Stolen refresh cookie survives password change.

**Fix:** Call `_revoke_all_user_refresh_tokens` after successful change-password.

---

### 12. MEDIUM — Login rate limit only; no account lockout

**Files:** `backend/app/core/rate_limit.py` ~L24–42; `backend/app/api/v1/auth.py` login ~L78; `backend/app/services/auth.py` `authenticate_user` ~L24–46

Per-IP sliding window, in-process (weak with multiple workers). No per-account lockout / backoff.

**Fix:** Track failed attempts per identifier; lock or exponential backoff; shared store if multi-worker.

---

### 13. MEDIUM — Object keys hotel-prefixed; public URL risk if bucket is public

**Files:** `backend/app/integrations/storage/base.py` — `new_object_key` ~L214–216; `public_url` on local/S3 backends

Keys are `hotels/{hotel_id}/…`. Serving guest docs / staff photos / selfies / expense attachments / logos goes through authenticated APIs (good). If R2/B2 `public_base` points at a public bucket, knowing a key bypasses app auth. Local `public_url` is not mounted in `main.py` (dead), but S3 public base is real risk.

**Fix:** Private bucket + app proxy only (current pattern); never return raw public object URLs for PII.

---

### 14. LOW — Upload validation mostly solid

| Path                   | Size / type                       | Hotel key | Serve auth                                 |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| Guest docs             | 5MB; png/jpeg/webp                | yes       | `GUESTS_VIEW` + scoped guest             |
| Staff photo            | typed + size                      | yes       | scoped profile                             |
| Selfie upload          | typed + size                      | yes       | record scoped;**key trust issue #2** |
| Expenses               | typed + size                      | yes       | scoped expense                             |
| Gallery / payment logo | 5MB / 2MB + type (+ PIL for logo) | yes       | hotel-scoped permissions                   |

---

### 15. LOW — Permission gaps on mutators

Hotel business routers use `require_permissions(...)`. Only `auth.py` uses bare `get_current_user` / `get_tenant_context` for self-service (`change-password`, `PATCH /me`) — appropriate. No hotel mutating route found that skips permissions while using only `get_current_user`.

---

### 16. LOW / positive — `must_reset_password` is server-side

**File:** `backend/app/api/deps.py` — `get_tenant_context` ~L53–63

Enforced on all hotel-scoped deps; `/auth/me`, change-password, refresh intentionally use `get_current_user` only. Not frontend-only.

---

### 17. LOW / positive — Refresh rotation + reuse detection

**File:** `backend/app/services/auth.py` — `rotate_refresh_token` ~L74–129

Revokes old token, detects reuse, revokes family, commits before raise. Solid.

---

### 18. LOW / positive — Numbering & double-booking locks

**Files:** `bookings.py` `_lock_rooms` + `next_booking_number` (`with_for_update`); `invoices.py` invoice sequence; `stay.py` registration / room locks; `expenses.py` recurring generation

Booking/invoice sequences and room overlap checks use row locks. Residual race mainly if any path allocates rooms without `_lock_rooms` (none found on create/replace/add).

---

### 19. LOW — Invoice helpers `db.get(Booking/Guest)` after hotel-scoped invoice

**File:** `backend/app/services/invoices.py` ~L322–351, ~L529–531

Safe if invoice was loaded with `hotel_id`; add `Booking.hotel_id == hotel_id` for defense in depth.

---

### 20. LOW — Super-admin guest by ID without hotel filter

**File:** `backend/app/services/super_admin.py` ~L746

Expected for platform admin; keep behind `require_super_admin` only.

---

### 21. INFO — Discounts cannot go negative via API schemas

`discount_amount` uses `Field(ge=0)` in booking/stay schemas; settlement uses `max(..., 0)`. Discount can zero the bill (full write-off) when authorized — product choice, not a negative-total bug.

---

## Tenant-isolation summary (ID-without-`hotel_id`)

| Location                                                                        | Risk                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `housekeeping.ensure_task_for_room` Room/Booking by id                        | Defense-in-depth / notification leak if IDs ever mismatch |
| `notifications.mark_read` by id                                               | Post-check; null-`hotel_id` edge case                   |
| `bookings` room reload by id after lock                                       | Mitigated by prior`_lock_rooms`                         |
| `guests` document by id + guest_id                                            | Guest already hotel-scoped                                |
| `attendance` selfie **key** (not DB id)                                 | Real cross-tenant file read if key injected               |
| Most`get_*` (booking, guest, payment, charge, invoice, expense, staff, leave) | Include`hotel_id` — good                               |

Primary IDOR-style concern after this pass is **#2 (selfie object key)**, not classic `db.get(Model, id)` on bookings/guests.

---

## Suggested fix order

1. Refund advance/deposit accounting (#1)
2. Validate selfie object-key prefix (#2)
3. Unify GST pricing (#3)
4. Harden or restrict `reverse_checkout` (#4)
5. Broaden `assert_transactions_allowed` (#5)
6. Auth session revoke + lockout (#11–12)
7. Defense-in-depth hotel_id on housekeeping/notifications/documents (#6–9)

No files were modified. I can turn any finding into a concrete patch next if you want.

# DigitalMyHotels Frontend Audit Report

Read-only audit of `C:\Users\BAPS\Documents\space\management\frontend\src`.
**Confirmed root cause of the client bug:** check-in drafts use a global `localStorage` key with no hotel (or user) scope.

**Mitigation note for React Query:** hotel switch in `partner-header.tsx` calls `queryClient.clear()`, which reduces cross-hotel RQ leakage on intentional switch. Unscoped keys remain HIGH (defense-in-depth / incomplete clear paths). `localStorage` / `sessionStorage` are **not** cleared on hotel switch.

---

## 1. Browser storage scoping

| #   | Sev                | Path                                                                              | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --- | ------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1.1 | **CRITICAL** | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\checkin\page.tsx`                                   | Key`dmh.checkinDrafts.v2` (+ legacy `dmh.checkinDraft.v1`). Hotel-scoped walk-in form data; key and payload have **no** `hotelId` / user id. Drafts from Hotel A appear under Hotel B. |
| 1.2 | **HIGH**     | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\checkin\page.tsx`                                   | Key`dmh.checkin.selectedBookingId` in **sessionStorage**. Booking id from Hotel A can be restored after switching hotels in the same tab.                                                  |
| 1.3 | **MEDIUM**   | `frontend\src\app\(super-admin)\admin\add-hotel\page.tsx`                       | Key`dmh.addHotelDraft`. Admin wizard draft, global. Cross-admin/session bleed on shared browsers.                                                                                                |
| 1.4 | **LOW**      | `frontend\src\components\tour\product-tour.tsx`                                 | Key`dmh.tourDone.v1`. UI preference; not tenant data, but not user-scoped.                                                                                                                       |
| 1.5 | **LOW**      | `frontend\src\components\subscription\subscription-gate.tsx`                    | Key`dmh.expiredModalShown` (sessionStorage). UI flag only.                                                                                                                                       |
| 1.6 | OK (context)       | `frontend\src\lib\auth\auth-context.tsx`                                        | `dmh.activeHotelId` — correctly stores active hotel.                                                                                                                                            |
| 1.7 | OK (auth)          | `frontend\src\lib\auth\session.ts`                                              | Access token /`/me` cache — session-scoped auth, not hotel tenant drafts.                                                                                                                       |
| 1.8 | OK (UI pref)       | `frontend\src\app\(super-admin)\admin\settings\page.tsx`, `admin-sidebar.tsx` | Customers-directory flag — admin UI preference.                                                                                                                                                   |

---

## 2. React Query key scoping (`app/(partner)/**`)

Hotel switch clears the cache, but these **hotel-scoped** queries omit `activeHotelId` from `queryKey`:

| #   | Sev              | Path                                                   | Query key                                                                                                                                                   | Issue                                                       |
| --- | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| 2.1 | **HIGH**   | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\current-guests\page.tsx` | `["booking", booking_id, "detail"]`, `["booking-guests", …]`, `["payments", …, "stay-dialog"]`, `["guest", guestId]`, `["booking", booking_id]` | Stay/edit dialogs not hotel-keyed.                          |
| 2.2 | **HIGH**   | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\checkout\page.tsx`       | `["booking-for-checkout", booking_id]`, `["checkout-quote", booking_id, …]`                                                                            | Checkout booking/quote cache not hotel-keyed.               |
| 2.3 | **MEDIUM** | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\settings\page.tsx`       | Invalidates`["hotel-qr-png"]` **without** `activeHotelId` (reads elsewhere use `["hotel-qr-png", activeHotelId]`)                               | Inconsistent scoping; relies on partial match /`clear()`. |
| 2.4 | **MEDIUM** | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\plan\page.tsx`           | Invalidates`["renewal-request-mine"]` without hotel id (query uses `["renewal-request-mine", activeHotelId]`)                                           | Same pattern.                                               |

**Mostly well-scoped:** `rooms`, `bookings`, `hk-tasks`, `payments` list keys, `smart-dashboard`, staff/attendance, expenses, reports, etc. include `activeHotelId`.

**OK:** `frontend\src\components\rooms\room-availability-picker.tsx` → `["room-availability", activeHotelId, checkIn, checkOut]`.

---

Primary form / filter actions with `h-7` / `h-8` / `h-9` (not tiny row icons):

| 6.6 | **MEDIUM** | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\team\page.tsx`            | Invite trigger`h-8`; form controls `h-8`/`h-9`.                                                                                                                 |
| 6.7 | **MEDIUM** | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\current-guests\page.tsx`  | Room transfer`<select>`s `h-8`.                                                                                                                                   |
| 6.8 | **MEDIUM** | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\edit-hotel\page.tsx`      | Several room/service selects`h-9`.                                                                                                                                  |

**Good examples:** staff attendance filters, expenses filter bar, advance-bookings CTA, checkout guest select — use `h-[42px]`.

---

## 7. Error handling (silent failures)

| #   | Sev              | Path                                                  | Finding                                                                                                                                       |
| --- | ---------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 7.1 | **HIGH**   | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\notifications\page.tsx` | `markOne` / `markAll` mutations: **no `onError`**. Failed mark-read → UI unchanged, no toast.                                    |
| 7.2 | **MEDIUM** | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\checkin\page.tsx`       | Document preview`fetch(…).catch(() => {/* silent */})` (~663, ~903). Intentional for empty tile, but failures are invisible.               |
| 7.3 | **MEDIUM** | `frontend\src\app\(partner)\checkin\page.tsx`       | Co-guest / primary-guest doc uploads:`.catch` → `console.warn` only (~2276, ~3345, ~3727). Booking can succeed while docs fail silently. |
| 7.4 | **LOW**    | Storage helpers                                       | `localStorage`/`sessionStorage` often swallow errors (acceptable for quota); draft save does toast on failure.                            |

Most `useMutation` usages elsewhere have `onError` + toast.

---

## Priority fix order

1. **CRITICAL:** Scope `dmh.checkinDrafts.v2` (and legacy) by `activeHotelId` (and ideally user id); filter on read.
2. **HIGH:** Scope `dmh.checkin.selectedBookingId` the same way.
3. **HIGH:** Add `activeHotelId` to unscoped query keys in current-guests / checkout.
4. **HIGH:** Broaden invalidation for HK / rooms / checkout / payments / reverse-checkout → `room-availability`, `smart-dashboard`, `payment-summary-today`.
5. **HIGH:** i18n for room-availability-picker, advance-booking, admin revenue; format raw statuses.
6. **MEDIUM:** 42px on primary form controls; notification mutation `onError`.

**Cursor**

Both audits are complete. Now assembling everything — the client's ~35 reported issues, my verified root causes, and both audits' findings — into the comprehensive implementation-plan file:

The complete master plan is written to `memory-bank/bugfixMasterPlan-2026-09-15.md` (committed as docs-only, `15920c0` — **zero code was changed**). It merges four sources: the client's ~35 issues, my own verified root causes, the [Backend bug audit](1b4d0c6a-b1d2-4552-8c4e-5ee56e68a62b) (21 findings), and the [Frontend bug audit](7c8b8eb6-c11d-4418-b6e4-53273fd6f187) (7 categories).

Beyond the client's list, the audits surfaced **new bugs the client hasn't found yet**, now in the plan:

- **Security-grade:** attendance selfie object-keys can be injected to read another hotel's files; password change doesn't revoke existing sessions; no per-account login lockout.
- **Money-grade:** refunds can exceed the advance and silently never touch the security deposit; reversing a checkout leaves the payment, late-fee ledger entry, and invoice all standing; booking totals are computed without GST while checkout/invoices include it — which is very likely the *actual* root of the client's "due amount wrong" and "payment amount wrong" complaints (three client symptoms, one cause).
- **Sync-grade:** a full mutation→missing-invalidation map (the housekeeping staleness the client reported is one instance of eight).
- **Consistency:** a concrete file list of unscoped query keys, hardcoded English, raw `snake_case` statuses, and 42px violations.

The plan is organized into 8 parts with 5 phases, each fix carrying its root cause, approach, and the logic risks you asked me to think through (e.g., why draft migration can misattribute once per device, why GST "not showing" must be reproduced against the hotel's mode before touching anything, why invoice history must never be renumbered).

**Six decisions in the plan need the client's/your answer before the respective items start** — most importantly: should expired hotels be blocked from login entirely, or allowed in but locked to the plan/renewal screen (my recommendation, otherwise renewal is impossible in-product)? The others cover cross-hotel guest search scope, old-draft migration, team-limit counting, same-day occupied-room blocking, and server-side draft document uploads. Answer those and Phase 1 can begin.

Right — let me verify the audits' load-bearing claims against the code myself (audits can be wrong), and stress-test the plan against cross-cutting scenarios: mobile, hotel switching, concurrency, storage, and real hotel-operations edge cases. Verifying the critical claims first:

Refund bug and selfie-key injection **confirmed first-hand** (deposit never reduced at line 459; client-supplied key stored verbatim and served unchecked). Continuing verification:

Done — the plan is now cross-verified rather than trusted, and it changed in three meaningful ways as a result (committed `4228a92`, still zero implementation).

**Every load-bearing audit claim was re-checked in the actual code.** Five confirmed first-hand with exact line numbers: the refund that never touches the security deposit, the selfie-key injection, GST-free booking totals (in four places), expired hotels editing/cancelling bookings, and the hotel-switch cache clear. Two audit claims turned out to be **overstated and were downgraded**: the sessionStorage booking-id "leak" is actually neutralized because the stored id is matched against the current hotel's booking list before use, and the notification `mark_read` gap only exists for rows no code path creates. Both fixes stay in the plan as hygiene, but they're no longer urgent — exactly the kind of correction you asked for.

**The scenario stress-test found real problems in my own plan:**

1. **Expired hotels with guests currently checked in** — my original Part 2 design (block everything) would have trapped in-house guests: no checkout, no due collection, no invoice. The plan now blocks by *operation class*: new business (bookings, check-ins, advance bookings) is blocked, but winding down existing stays (checkout, payment collection, invoicing) stays allowed. This is the difference between a correct product and an angry hotel with stranded guests.
2. **A brand-new bug found during the scenario pass (Part 1.8):** switching hotels in the header doesn't unmount the page — a check-in form filled under Hotel A can be *submitted* under Hotel B. Same family as the draft leak, previously unknown.
3. **Mobile-specific traps documented:** the idle-logout timer must be timestamp-based (mobile browsers freeze `setTimeout` in the background — a naive timer would either never fire or misfire), the room picker's "coming free" section needs to stay visible on small screens once same-day blocking moves rooms there, and the 2× ID viewer must not break pinch gestures.
4. **Ordering constraint made explicit:** draft-document uploads (Part 4.2) are *gated* on production storage being verified on B2 first — otherwise draft documents get lost exactly like the Aadhaar photos the client reported.

The matrix also covers concurrency (team-limit enforcement must count inside a locked transaction or two simultaneous adds beat the cap), hotel-timezone handling for the auto-checkout job, offline tolerance for draft restore, and migration safety rules (no deletions for over-cap teams, no invoice renumbering, zero-downtime columns).

The plan file now has: root causes → fixes → traceability to every client sheet row → verified evidence → scenario coverage → the nine open decisions (Q1–Q9). It's ready to execute the moment you answer the decision questions — Q1 (expired login behavior) and Q3 (old draft migration) are the only two blocking Phase 1.

**Items shipped, one by one:**

5. **Expiry enforcement, backend (Part 2/S1)** — the tenant dependency now blocks all mutations for hotels past expiry + grace, *except* the wind-down whitelist (checkouts, payments, invoices, charges, renewal requests), so in-house guests can still be settled — the scenario that would have trapped guests under a naive block. New integration test proves: mutation blocked with `subscription_expired`, reads still work, renewal request still works. Full suite: 217 passed, only the 3 documented pre-existing failures.

**Assumptions I attacked and what I found:**

1. **Does the backend whitelist match reality?** The wind-down prefixes (`/checkouts`, `/payments`, `/invoices`, `/charges`, `/subscriptions`) were checked against every actual `APIRouter(prefix=...)` declaration — all five match exactly, and the checkout page's real mutation calls (`POST /api/v1/checkouts/{id}/quote`, `POST /api/v1/checkouts`) land inside the whitelist.
2. **Can the frontend panel and backend blocking disagree?** No — `/subscriptions/me` calls `refresh_status()` before returning, so the status the panel reads is computed from the same expiry+grace math the blocking uses. A hotel is never blocked while the UI says "active", or vice versa.
3. **Mobile:** the wind-down panel (z-150) covers the floating tab bar (z-35) — no navigation escape hatch on phones. The suspended overlay (z-200) still correctly outranks both.
4. **Selfie validation binding:** `self_check_in` resolves the profile via `get_or_create_own_profile`, so the expected prefix (`hotels/<hotel>/staff/selfies/<profile.user_id>/`) is guaranteed to match keys minted by `upload_selfie` for that same user — no legitimate check-in can be rejected.
5. **Draft migration re-read found a flaw:** adopted legacy drafts were persisted *before* id backfill, so they'd get fresh random ids on every reload — making discard-by-id unstable across tabs. Fixed: ids are now backfilled before persisting.
6. **i18n parity:** scripted comparison of the `plan` namespace — all 6 new keys present in both English and Hindi, zero namespace drift.
7. **Wind-down side effects considered:** checkout still sets rooms to cleaning-required internally (service layer, not HTTP — unaffected by the block), but housekeeping *completion* is blocked for expired hotels — acceptable and consistent with "non-operational". Edit Stay on the allowed Current Guests page will get a clear `subscription_expired` error toast from the backend rather than silently working — acceptable.

**Final green board:** `tsc` clean, expiry + attendance suites 14/14 passed, lints show only pre-existing warnings, and the earlier full-suite run stands at 217 passed with only the 3 documented pre-existing failures.

Phase 1 is verified solid. Next up when you're ready: Phase 2 — the check-in rework (guest identity state machine, autofill with documents, draft schema v3, same-day room blocking), same one-item-at-a-time protocol.

**Phase 2 is shipped and pushed** (`36f5647` → `f1dc42f`), implemented item by item under the strict protocol with per-item verification and one deliberate deferral. What landed:

**Guest identity flow (§5.1)** — the client's critical "blank / hidden / no update" cluster, three separate root causes fixed:

- *Blank tiles after Create Guest:* document uploads were fire-and-forget while the document list was fetched immediately — the list always came back empty. Uploads are now awaited; failures surface as a warning toast instead of silence.
- *Blank tiles after Confirm (co-guests):* the resolved card remounts the tiles, losing queued previews. New `initialFile` prop feeds the queued photo back in — previews survive the confirm.
- *"Hides customer":* the resolved card now shows a read-only summary grid (gender, DOB, address, city/state, pincode, masked ID) so confirming never visually discards what was typed.
- *"No update button":* the icon-only pencil became a labeled **Edit** button, and the edit form's action is now **Update Guest**. Plus the "++ Add Room" double icon is fixed (the label itself contained a "+").

**OCR autofill (§4.3)** — found a genuine argument-order bug: the co-guest/create tiles passed the document *side* where the function expects the *ID type*, so back faces never reached the Aadhaar address parser and the autofill banner never appeared — the client's exact "Autofill button not showing, both guest flows". Fixed, and OCR failures now toast instead of failing silently.

**Draft restore (§4.2)** — drafts now serialize additional guests (all text data, pending-creation forms, Form C). On restore, entries mount directly in the resolved state; returning guests re-fetch their profile and saved documents automatically. Queued photo *files* still can't survive localStorage — that's §4.2b, which I deliberately deferred until you confirm production storage is on B2 (otherwise draft photos would be lost server-side too, same as the Aadhaar bug).

**Same-day room blocking (§5.2, backend)** — the availability service now excludes physically-occupied rooms and mid-cleaning rooms for stays starting *today* (computed in the hotel's timezone, per the guard-rail), moving them to "coming free". Once the guest checks out, the room returns instantly. Future-dated bookings keep today's behavior, so advance booking is unaffected — proven by a new regression test plus the day-use, lifecycle, and concurrency suites (16/16).

**ID readability (§4.4)** — the crop frame grows ~1.8× on desktop (clamped to viewport; phones unchanged). Deliberately grew the *frame*, not the zoom — a zoom bump would have silently cropped the card edges when staff hit Done immediately.

**Phase gate:** production build green; full backend suite 218 passed with only the 3 documented pre-existing failures.

Next in the plan is Phase 3 (money integrity: auto-invoice at checkout, unified GST-aware pricing, refund/deposit allocation, reverse-checkout hardening) — plus §4.2b once you've confirmed the Render storage health check shows B2.

6. **Crop frame** — growth is applied to the *frame* with viewport clamps (`≤ width−48`, `≤ height−240`), floor of 1 so phones keep today's exact layout; confirmed the memo runs client-side only.

**The flaw found:** my draft v3 serialization stored the co-guest's **full ID number in plaintext localStorage** via `newForm`. That violates the master-context rule on minimizing sensitive identity data — and the existing code had deliberately never drafted the primary guest's ID number, which was the tell. Fixed: `id_number` is stripped on save; staff re-enter it after restore, exactly like the primary-guest flow. Typechecked, committed, pushed.

Phase 2 stands verified. Next up per the plan: Phase 3 — money integrity (auto-invoice at checkout, unified GST-aware pricing across booking/Edit Stay/checkout, refund vs deposit allocation, reverse-checkout hardening), each with its own tests. Ready when you are.

**Phase 3 (money integrity) is fully shipped and pushed** (`df1399f` → `6ff1469`), six items implemented one-by-one under the protocol, each with tests. What changed:

**Refund allocation (§3.2)** — refunds now reduce the advance first and the security deposit for any remainder, with the split recorded in the audit trail. Previously a refund larger than the advance left the deposit untouched while cash physically left the hotel. Regression test proves ₹600 against ₹300 advance + ₹500 deposit lands as 300/300, and over-refunds still reject.

**Auto-invoice at checkout (§3.4)** — the client's "invoice not generated" and "invoice ID wrong" in one stroke. Invoices are now created *inside* the atomic checkout transaction, so every completed stay has one and numbers follow checkout order going forward. The checkout response returns the invoice id; Print/Download/Email use it directly; the manual Generate dialog now treats "already exists" as a friendly success; and clicking **View** in the invoice list smoothly scrolls to the preview (the client's "View click → scroll down"). The two tests that asserted the old lazy behavior were updated to assert the new contract, including no-duplicate on retry.

**Reversal hardening (§3.3)** — reversing a checkout now auto-cancels its invoice (reason recorded), credits back the late-fee ledger debit, and is *blocked* if a payment was recorded after checkout (with a one-minute margin so the checkout's own same-transaction collection never false-positives). Two new tests, including proof that a re-checkout issues a fresh invoice.

**Unified GST pricing (§3.1)** — the root of "incorrect payment due amount": booking totals were GST-free while checkout/invoices were GST-aware. All four booking-pricing sites (create, date edits, room replacement, room addition) now use the same mode-aware GST engine — so Edit Stay's due, booking lists, checkout, and the invoice show one number, for all three GST modes including tax-inclusive hotels. Two old test baselines were updated with explanation, plus a new regression test.

**Card/Others everywhere (§3.6)** — those tiles in the client's screenshot didn't exist anywhere; now the payments summary, expenses summary, reports payment split, and dashboard Today's Collection all carry Credit/Debit Card and Others buckets, rendered on all four screens (dashboard shows all four modes always), English + Hindi.

**Powered-by branding (§3.8)** — new `show_powered_by` setting (migration `aa15b9c27e01`, applied and verified locally — the first id I picked collided with an existing revision, caught by the alembic cycle check before commit), footer on the invoice card and PDF, super-admin toggle on the hotel-edit Settings section, and the booking number now shows on the invoice preview per the client's "Add below BK-0023" note.

**Phase gate:** production build green, full backend suite **222 passed** with only the 3 documented pre-existing failures. §3.5 (quote snapshot) was consciously deferred as low-urgency — recorded in the plan.

Also still waiting on the production storage health check from your side for the photos issue. Ready to continue.

Phase 3 reverification complete — it caught **three real problems**, two of them serious, all fixed and pushed (`4d4ea7a`). This is exactly why the reverify step matters:

**Finding 1 — a regression my own §3.1 created (serious).** Making booking totals GST-inclusive broke an assumption in the booked check-in screen: its payment breakdown added *approximate GST on top of the booking total*, which now already contains room GST — the desk would have seen room GST **counted twice** in the balance. Fixed: the approximation now applies only to the new extras being added at the desk (which get real GST when charged); the walk-in preview was verified to be correct as-is since its room rates are ex-GST until the booking is created.

**Finding 2 — a hole in my §3.4 conflict handling (serious).** The check-in success screen lets staff generate an invoice mid-stay. My checkout code *reused* that invoice on conflict — but a mid-stay invoice can't contain checkout charges or late fees, so the printed invoice would disagree with the final bill: literally the client's "payment amount wrong, print and modal" complaint reproduced through a different door. Fixed: checkout now cancels the stale invoice with reason "Superseded by the final checkout invoice" and regenerates, so the invoice of record always equals the settlement. New regression test proves the old one is cancelled and the final one includes the checkout charge.

**Finding 3 — minor:** the reports CSV export was missing the new card/others buckets; added.

**Interactions attacked and held:**

- Wind-down × auto-invoice: checkout's own subscription assert runs before invoice generation, so no new failure mode for expired hotels; both `/checkouts` and `/invoices` are whitelisted.
- Reversal payment-guard margin: the checkout's own collection (Python timestamp) vs the checkout row (transaction timestamp) stays inside the 1-minute margin — no false blocks; a genuinely later payment (tested at +10 min) still blocks.
- §3.2 flows correctly through `settle_booking_amounts` — verified it counts advance + deposit, so the deposit reduction updates due/status properly.
- Current Guests due is computed independently from rates — unaffected by the totals change, still correct.
- PDF footer defaults ON when a hotel has no settings row; dashboard's four payment bars always sum to 100%.

This is the final full-sheet audit: first a deep Phase-5 verification, then item-by-item justification of every client point, honestly flagging anything still open. Starting with the items I haven't yet proven in code — the temp-password flow (10.3), the renewal payment flow's transaction reference (Part 9), and the two-modals rule (10.9):

Confirmed — the modal never asks for the transaction ID. Implementing the missing piece of the client's payment-verification flow:

Implementing all three layers of the transaction-ID capture:

Now the plan-page modal (required transaction-ID field) and the SA table display:

Part 9's missing piece is closed and pushed (`530fd18`). Updating the plan with the audit outcome, then the full justification:

Deep audit complete. It caught **one real gap in Part 9** (now fixed and pushed: the payment modal never collected the UPI transaction ID and the super admin couldn't see it — the exact core of the client's payment-verification requirement; a required transaction-reference field now feeds through to the super-admin approval table). Here is the honest per-item justification of the entire client sheet:

**Multi-hotel isolation (the critical one):** drafts were leaking via a global browser-storage key — now per-hotel keys with one-time migration; pages remount on hotel switch so a half-filled form can't submit under another hotel; the selfie-key injection (cross-tenant file read) is closed with a test. Guest data is now the *only* shared thing, exactly as the client demanded: cross-hotel search works by full phone only, results masked, full details + Aadhaar photos copied only via explicit, phone-proofed, audited Import. The old tenant test was upgraded to prove foreign direct-access still 404s.

**Expired hotels (Raj Place):** was cosmetic-only; now server-enforced past expiry+grace — new business blocked at the API for every endpoint, with a wind-down whitelist so in-house guests can still be checked out and paid up. Frontend shows a non-dismissible panel outside those pages with Renew/Checkouts/Sign-out and the **Pending** renewal badge the client asked for. Verified end-to-end by an integration test.

**Photos/documents cluster:** the create-guest upload race (fire-and-forget uploads vs immediate document fetch) is fixed — that was "uploaded Aadhaar but not show"; queued previews survive Confirm; autofill's missing button was a genuine argument-order bug (document side passed as ID type, killing the back-face parser) — fixed with failure toasts; draft restore now round-trips co-guest text data and rehydrates saved documents; the ID viewer frame is ~1.8× bigger (frame, not zoom — zoom would have cropped cards). One dependency remains yours: production must be on B2 (`/storage/health`), or photos die at the storage layer regardless of code.

**Money cluster:** invoices auto-generate inside checkout (fixes "invoice not generated" and makes numbering chronological — "Invoice ID wrong"); stale mid-stay invoices are superseded at checkout (the "print vs modal amounts differ" mechanism); booking totals are GST-aware via the shared engine, so Edit Stay's due now equals checkout and invoice ("incorrect payment due amount"); refunds allocate advance→deposit; reversal cancels the invoice and reverses late fees; Card/Others buckets exist on Payment Details, Hotel Expenses, Dashboard collection, and Reports (they existed nowhere before — the screenshot was a mock); "Powered by DigitalMyHotels" is on card+PDF with the per-hotel super-admin toggle; booking numbers show under Payment Details and the invoice preview; "GST not showing" is *correct behavior* for No-GST/Included-by-Hotel hotels under the three-mode implementation — it must be re-tested against an Including-Customer hotel before being called a bug.

**Sync & flows:** the housekeeping "Cleaning Soon needs refresh" class is closed by shared invalidation across nine pages plus 30-second polling; same-day check-ins can no longer select physically occupied or mid-cleaning rooms (hotel-local dates; future/advance bookings unchanged, per the availability tests); the guest identity screen keeps a read-only summary after Confirm with labeled Edit/Update buttons — the blank/hide/no-update trio each had a distinct root cause, all fixed; the double "++" was a literal "+" inside the Add Room label.

**Platform features:** team cap of 5 enforced under a row lock, super admin exempt (your clarification) and able to raise limits at setup or later — the 7-member hotel keeps its members but can't add; idle logout at 15 minutes, timestamp-based so backgrounded phones log out on return; temp-password flow verified implemented end-to-end (forced change is server-enforced with an automatic redirect — if the client still sees it, I need their exact path); super-admin items from the earlier batch (revenue screen, recently-expired ≤5 days, expired-shows-Active, reset-password option, export CSV, plan-page link, edit-not-saving) all shipped on the 14th and await their re-test.

**Discipline & polish:** the shared input-discipline library (10-digit phones, per-type ID caps for Aadhaar/PAN/Passport/DL/Voter, live name capitalization — also enforced server-side), the shared status formatter ("Expiring Soon"), the 42px sweep, plans-page centering and styled CTAs, sidebar Staff-after-Money, team-menu width + "Edit Profile", vendor detail modal, per-cause location errors, and unsaved room types now selectable and resolved at save.

**Honestly still open (also recorded in the plan):** the SA add-hotel "not working" claim (backend lifecycle tests pass — I need the client's exact failure), the double-scroll screenshot (needs repro), auto-checkout-next-day (blocked on your Q9 decision about unpaid dues), the unlabeled "Wrong" screenshot, draft *photo* persistence (blocked on B2), and a handful of minor empty-state strings pending Hindi. Everything else on the sheet is implemented, tested (225-passing suite + green builds), and pushed through `0529c57`.

The screenshot exposes two real bugs in my dashboard collection row: the "Others" bar shows **100% when nothing was collected** (my remainder formula doesn't guard the zero-total day), and the label still says "Card / Other" — a stale name now that card has its own row. Verifying both:

Both confirmed. Fixing the zero-day 100% and the stale label:

Both problems in the circled "Today's Collection" card are fixed and pushed (`6e50d27`):

1. **The 100% bar on a ₹0 day** — the "Others" percentage was computed as the *remainder* (100 minus the other three), so on a day with zero collection it showed a full 100% bar next to ₹0, which is exactly what the screenshot captured. The remainder now only applies when money was actually collected; an empty day shows all four bars at 0%.
2. **The stale "Card / Other" label** — that was the old combined bucket name from before Credit/Debit Card got its own row, so the card was showing "Credit/Debit Card" *and* "Card / Other" together, which read as a duplicate. It's now simply "Others" (Hindi: "अन्य"), matching the payments and reports screens.

I also checked the reports page's payment split for the same remainder bug — it computes percentages from the sum of the four buckets with a zero-guard already, so the dashboard was the only place affected. Typecheck clean, committed, pushed.

One note on what the screenshot shows working correctly: the row order (Cash → UPI → Credit/Debit Card → Others → Total Collected) and all four modes being always visible is the layout the client requested earlier — that part is behaving as intended, the two rendering bugs just made it look broken on an empty day.

All green. Commit and push:

All three fixes are shipped in `916533a`. Here's exactly what each screenshot showed and what changed:

---

### Screenshot 1 (63563159) — Add New Hotel

Two annotations, two fixes:

**"Room Type Missing"** — The room inventory section had a `<select>` dropdown with 4 hardcoded options (Deluxe Suite, Standard Double, Standard Single, Premium Suite). Any custom name typed outside those options was silently ignored. Changed to a free-text `<Input>` so any room type name works. The mutation also previously created a *single* room type for **all** rooms using the first room's name with a hardcoded code `"STD"` — so 4 rooms with different types all ended up the same type. Now rooms are grouped by type name and a separate `RoomType` record is created for each unique name.

**"Add Hotel not working"** — Added `onMutate: () => setError(null)` so a stale red error banner from a previous failed attempt doesn't stick around confusingly on the next click.

---

### Screenshot 2 (63563463) — Add Hotel bottom

Two annotations, two fixes:

**"Remove spacing"** — The `<main>` had `!pb-44` (176px bottom padding) for the sticky footer clearance. The footer is only ~66px tall, so 176px created a visible blank white box. Reduced to `!pb-24` (96px) which is still generous but no longer looks like a broken empty section.

**"Double scroll show"** — The SA layout's `PageTransition` had `className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto"`. In CSS flexbox, `flex: 1` keeps `min-height: auto` which means the div grows to its *content* height — `overflow-y-auto` never fires because the container is never shorter than its content. Added `min-h-0` which overrides that default, allows the div to shrink to the allocated flex height, and lets `overflow-y-auto` create a single proper scroll. The outer `overflow-hidden` container then no longer leaks a second native scrollbar.

---

### Screenshot 3 (63535942) — Room 1010: OCCUPIED (Room Status) vs CLEANING REQUIRED (Housekeeping)

**Root cause:** checkout creates a housekeeping task (`status = cleaning_required`) and sets `room.status = CLEANING_REQUIRED`. When checkout is *reversed*, `room.status` goes back to `OCCUPIED` — but the housekeeping task was never cancelled, so housekeeping still showed "Cleaning Required" for an occupied room. Fixed in `reverse_checkout`: after restoring `room.status = OCCUPIED` for each room, all open housekeeping tasks for that room are now cancelled with the note "Auto-cancelled: checkout reversed". Verified by the existing 3-test `test_checkout_reversal.py` suite (all still passing).

Done — deep analysis complete, verified live on the master server, and the full plan is written to `memory-bank/roomStatusRedesignPlan-2026-09-15.md` (committed, **zero code changed**). Here's the TLDR:

## What I verified live (logged in as owner@sg.in)

The hotel has exactly two confirmed bookings: **BK-0020 (Sep 16–17)** and **BK-0021 (Sep 24–29)**. Today (Sep 15), rooms 108, 215, 217 show **RESERVED** on the Room Status grid — for a booking up to **9 days away** — while the availability API simultaneously returns those same three rooms as **available for tonight**. Your observation is completely real, and both behaviors are "working as coded," which is precisely the problem.

## Root cause

`room.status` is a *physical* state (occupied, cleaning, maintenance…), but `create_booking` writes a *calendar* fact into it — any confirmed booking immediately paints the room `RESERVED`, no matter how far away check-in is. One field is answering two different questions, and that produces six traceable bugs:

1. **Far-future paint** — room looks blocked for 9 days while sellable.
2. **Walk-in trap (worst one)** — the picker offers room 108 for tonight (no date overlap), but when staff actually books it, `create_booking`'s same-day check rejects `reserved` rooms → 409 error. The picker offers what booking then refuses.
3. **Status drift** — with two future bookings on one room, cancelling one flips the room to Available even though the other still exists; a stored flag can never stay truthful.
4. **Timezone bug** — booking creation's "is this same-day?" check uses the *server's UTC date*, not hotel time (availability already uses hotel time; booking didn't get that fix).
5. **Misleading counts** — "Reserved: 3" on the stat card implies 3 arrivals now.
6. **"Occupied but bookable" confusion** — occupied rooms *are* correctly bookable for future dates (the guest leaves first), but the UI only says "Occupied now" without explaining why it's offered.

## Recommended solution (matches your instinct exactly)

**Never store "reserved" — derive it.** Room keeps only physical statuses; reservation info is computed from the bookings table at read time:

- Physically free + guest arriving *today* → **"Reserved (today) · arrives 14:00"**
- Physically free + future booking → **"Available"** with a ribbon **"Reserved from Sep 24"** — sellable until then
- Occupied + free before your requested dates → **"Occupied now · departs Sep 16 — free for your dates"**

This kills all six bugs structurally (no cron, no drift — double-booking safety already lives entirely in the date-overlap check, never in the status field), plus a one-line data migration converting existing `reserved` rows to `available`.

## Three questions for the client before implementing

1. Should the "Reserved" stat card count only *today's* arrivals (my recommendation) or all future reservations?
2. Should the "Reserved from …" ribbon always show the next booking, however far out? (Recommended: yes, it's free.)
3. Day-use gap on an arrival day — keep blocked (current behavior) or allow with warning?

Estimated effort is ~2 days total (1 backend + migration + tests, 1 frontend + i18n), risk low. Say the word and I'll implement it.

**Cursor**

Good question — that's the one place where the two mental models collide, so it needs an explicit rule. My recommendation:

**A room that's free now with a future booking belongs under the "Available" filter — because it *is* sellable tonight — with the ribbon still shown on its card. The "Reserved" filter narrows to rooms whose guest arrives *today*.**

Reasoning:

1. **Filters should answer the question staff are actually asking.** Someone clicking "Available" means "what can I sell right now?" — room 108 (booked from Sep 24) is a correct answer to that, so hiding it there would be a lie in the other direction from today's bug. Someone clicking "Reserved" means "who's arriving / which rooms are held today?" — a Sep-24 booking is noise in that view.
2. **Counts must add up.** The stat cards partition all 16 rooms into buckets that sum to the total. If a room appeared in both Available *and* Reserved, the numbers would no longer add up and the client would file that as a new bug (they already flagged "Room Status wrong count" once). Single-membership keeps the math honest.
3. **The upcoming booking isn't hidden — it moves to the ribbon.** In the Available filter, room 108's card still shows "Reserved from Sep 24" in gold, so staff filtering for sellable rooms simultaneously see the sell-by constraint. That's strictly more information than today, where the room just vanishes into the Reserved bucket for 9 days.
4. **Optional nicety if the client wants a one-click "all future reservations" view:** the Reserved filter view can render two sections — "Arriving today" on top and "Upcoming reservations" below it (still counted under Available in the stat cards). That gives the manager's planning view without corrupting the counts. I'd propose this as a small follow-up rather than the default, and the same logic applies to the availability picker's "Reserved" chip: it would match rooms with *any* upcoming booking, since in the picker context you're planning around dates anyway.

I've recorded this decision in the plan document:

Decision recorded in the plan. To summarize the filter behavior under the new model with a concrete example — room 108, free today, booked Sep 24:

| Filter clicked                    | Shows room 108?            | What its card says                                   |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| All                               | Yes                        | Available badge + gold "Reserved from Sep 24" ribbon |
| Available                         | Yes                        | Same — sellable now, constraint visible             |
| Reserved                          | No (only today's arrivals) | —                                                   |
| Occupied / Cleaning / Maintenance | No                         | —                                                   |

And on Sep 24 itself, room 108 automatically moves to the Reserved filter as "Reserved (today) · arrives 14:00" — no cron, no stored flag, just derived from the booking dates.

Still one open question for the client (the "Reserved" stat card count — today's arrivals only, or all future), then the plan is ready to implement whenever you give the go.

## What changed, in plain terms

**"Reserved" is no longer stored on the room — it's computed from bookings every time you look.** That single change kills every bug we found, because the stored flag was the root of all of them.

### Backend

- Booking creation, room swap, and add-room **no longer paint the room Reserved**. Double-booking safety was always in the date-overlap check — that's untouched.
- The rooms list now derives, per room, hour-accurate facts in one batched query: *guest arriving today (+ time)*, *next future booking (date + time)*, *current guest departing today (+ time)*.
- The **walk-in trap is fixed and regression-tested**: booking a room tonight that has a Sep-24 reservation now succeeds instead of 409ing. The same-day blocked-set now exactly mirrors the picker's rules (previously the picker offered `cleaning_required` rooms the booking API rejected).
- The **timezone bug is fixed**: same-day decisions use hotel-local today everywhere, not the server's UTC date (bookings made 00:00–05:30 IST were misclassified before).
- **Migration** converts existing `reserved` rows to `available` — on the master server, rooms 108/215/217 will immediately show correctly after deploy.

### What staff will see (using your live data)

| Room                             | Before                              | After                                                                                                              |
| -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 108 (booked Sep 24)              | "Reserved" for 9 straight days      | "Available" + gold ribbon**"Reserved from 24/09, 14:00"**                                                    |
| 217 (guest arrives tomorrow)     | "Reserved" today                    | "Available" + ribbon today; flips to**"Reserved (Today) · Arrives 14:00"** automatically on the arrival day |
| 216 (occupied, departs today)    | "Occupied"                          | "Occupied" +**"Departs today 11:00"**                                                                        |
| Picker offering room 108 tonight | Bare "Reserved" chip, booking fails | **"Booked from 24/09, 14:00 — free for your dates"**, booking succeeds                                      |

The dashboard chips and the Room Status page now share one bucketing helper, so their counts can never disagree, and the six cards always sum to the room total. Old dashboard deep-links (`?filter=cleaning_required`) still work via a legacy mapping.

### Also cleaned up

The full test run surfaced 3 *pre-existing* stale tests (I verified they fail without my change too) — expectations written against contracts that later batches deliberately changed (suspended hotels now block at login, password-reset returns 404 for unknown accounts since it surfaces the phone, expense summary gained the card/other buckets). All three updated to the current intended contracts.

One deploy note: the Alembic migration (`cc37d1e49a23`) runs on deploy and clears the stale Reserved paint on production instantly.

**Cursor**

Let me verify exactly what the current code does before answering — checking the draft save/restore logic for co-guests in the check-in page:

Yes — that was solved in Phase 2 (pushed on the 15th), and I just re-verified the exact code paths. Here's precisely what happens now:

**On "Save Draft":** every co-guest's text data is serialized into the hotel-scoped draft — their guest ID, name, phone, the *entire typed form* for a new not-yet-created co-guest (via `newForm`), and their Form C foreign-guest data if any. Before the fix, co-guests weren't in the draft at all, which was the client's "When I Restore, Additional Guests Detail not showing."

**On "Restore":** the co-guest entries mount directly as resolved summary cards (not empty search boxes), and then it depends on the type of co-guest:

- **Existing guest** (was picked from search): a rehydration effect re-fetches their full profile via `/autofill` plus their saved documents from the server, so the detail grid **and the Aadhaar front/back/selfie tiles fill back in automatically** — those photos live in B2, not in the browser.
- **New guest** (form filled but not yet saved to the server): all their typed details come back exactly as entered — name, ID, address, everything. The **one thing that does not survive is locally attached photos that were never uploaded**: a browser physically cannot store `File` objects in localStorage, so staff re-attach those photos after restore. This is documented in the code and in the plan as a hard browser limitation, not a bug we skipped.

Also relevant: money state (advance received, rate edits, service amounts) round-trips too, and the draft stays in the list after restore — it's only deleted when the check-in actually completes or staff explicitly discard it.

So if the client re-tests: co-guest details **will** show after restore in all cases; saved Aadhaar photos will show for existing/created guests; only never-uploaded local photo attachments need re-attaching. If they report details still missing after this, I'd need their exact steps — the most likely culprit would be a draft saved *before* the fix deployed (old drafts predating the fix simply never had co-guest data written into them, so there's nothing to restore).

## The truth about the list

The client re-pasted their **entire sheet**, but I pulled down and examined every screenshot. The sheet splits into three groups:

**Group 1 — already fixed, awaiting their re-test (~45 items).** Everything from GST modes, capitalization, exports, expired-hotel handling, autofill/Aadhaar display, housekeeping sync, payment buckets, Powered-by branding, team cap, idle logout, invoice generation, plan-page centering, cross-hotel search, etc. — all shipped across the 14th–15th. Several of their screenshots literally predate the fixes (one shows the old "Card / Other 100%" dashboard bug that was fixed yesterday).

**Group 2 — 12 genuinely NEW issues from their latest re-test (screenshot IDs 63589655–63616967 + two older stragglers). All fixed today:**

1. **CSV export missing ID/contact** — the export reused the *masked* on-screen list. New audited backend endpoint exports full phone + decrypted ID (every export logged to the audit trail).
2. **Room Types missing delete icon** — no DELETE endpoint existed. Added soft-delete (blocked with a clear "used by N rooms" message while rooms reference the type) + trash icons in both partner Edit Hotel and SA hotel edit.
3. **"DigitalMyHotels" flash while loading** — sidebar now shows a skeleton until the hotel name loads.
4. **"GST Update not working"** — they typed a 13-char GSTIN; GSTIN must be exactly 15. Now validated *before* submit with a plain-language message, auto-uppercase, and a format hint.
5. **42px + 20px-padding buttons "all screens both systems"** — changed the shared Button component default itself (`h-8 px-2.5` → `h-[42px] px-5`), so every default button in both systems complies at once. Compact `sm`/`xs`/icon variants untouched.
6. **Confusing CANCELLED invoice** — that's the supersede-at-checkout design working; cancelled rows now display *why* (e.g. "superseded at checkout").
7. **International guest phones** — 10-digit hard cap raised to E.164's 15 digits on guest fields (frontend + backend `normalize_phone`, which was silently truncating foreign numbers to the last 10). Staff/vendor/hotel fields stay Indian 10-digit.
8. **No-GST hotel showing GST column** in Payment Details — hidden now.
9. **"Reload page then system show"** — real bug: check-in used query key `hotel-gst` while settings invalidated `gst-settings`. Unified; GST mode changes now propagate live. Also killed the empty 5th cell in the payment strip for no-GST hotels.
10. **Daily Closing "Not Show Detail"** — history rows now show check-ins/outs, revenue, cash, UPI, expenses, dues, and notes.
11. **"Currently open 2 models"** — the Print row action no longer stacks the detail modal under the print window; it loads invisibly and closes after printing.
12. **Next-day auto-checkout** — implemented (this reverses the earlier "alert-only" decision, per their message). Conservative money-safe rules: only stays a **full hotel-local day** past checkout AND **fully paid** get auto-checked-out through the real checkout service (invoice, housekeeping, audit identical to manual); stays with dues are never auto-closed — humans collect money. Three new tests prove all three paths.

**Group 3 — one item needs their clarification:** "Rooms select is wrong as per our discussion" (63616725). Their screenshot actually shows the *new* reserved-ribbon design working exactly as we agreed two days ago — cleaning-soon rooms selectable (cleaned before arrival) and future-booked rooms offered with "free for your dates". If they mean something different, I need them to say which room in that screenshot should not have been selectable and why.

**Verification:** 236/236 backend tests, TypeScript clean, production build green, pushed. One heads-up: the whole-suite run initially failed because Docker Desktop (test Postgres) had shut down — restarted it, nothing to do with the code.

**Draft photo persistence — implemented**

Browsers cannot store `File` objects in localStorage. On **Save Draft**, queued co-guest photos now upload first to hotel-scoped B2 (`hotels/{hotelId}/draft-docs/…`). The draft only keeps the object keys. Restore downloads them back into the tiles. Discard, a completed check-in, overflowing the 10-draft cap, or a **7-day** abandoned-draft sweep deletes the objects.

Isolation is enforced twice: the key must start with that hotel’s prefix, and a `guest_draft_documents` row must exist for that hotel. Hotel B gets 404 on Hotel A’s key. Re-saving a restored draft reuses existing keys instead of uploading duplicates.

**Not live yet.** This is still local/uncommitted. Production B2 is ready, but the new endpoints and migration (`dd48e2f5ab34`) are not on Render until you commit and deploy. After deploy, `alembic upgrade head` (already in Render’s start command) creates the table.

Audit done, and everything it found is either fixed and verified or explicitly documented as accepted. Full picture:

## What the audit covered

Two deep audits ([backend](ec753a03-2cce-4db6-b8b2-66321ca52ddc), [frontend](5b466c1a-345d-4945-bf36-973d4dbdcb06)) looked for the whole "data never actually persisted" bug class: local-disk writes on Render's ephemeral disk, every upload feature's path to B2, browser localStorage/sessionStorage misuse, in-memory server state, database schema drift against migrations, and transaction ordering between B2 and the DB. I re-verified every load-bearing claim in the code myself before touching anything.

## Fixed now (all verified)

**Critical — draft photos could delete each other.** If staff restored a draft and saved it again, both drafts pointed at the *same* B2 photo objects. Discarding, evicting, or completing check-in on one destroyed the other's photos. All four deletion sites now skip keys still referenced by any surviving draft.

**High — three more real losses:**

- Restored co-guest photo tiles rendered blank, and attaching one new photo silently wiped the restored ones (the card's internal state ignored restored files). Now seeded correctly.
- Co-guest ID photo uploads during check-in were fire-and-forget — a failure only hit the browser console while the draft backup was deleted anyway, losing the photo entirely. Uploads are now awaited; failures show a warning toast and the draft is kept so photos remain recoverable.
- Super-admin Add Hotel "Save Draft" silently dropped the logo and gallery photos (Files can't serialize). The admin now gets an explicit notice (en+hi).

**Medium — backend durability:**

- A missing logo object in B2 **blocked the hotel from updating its UPI payment config entirely** — the code caught an exception type the storage layer never raises. Fixed.
- Rotating the encryption key would have permanently bricked every stored guest ID and UPI ID. Added `MultiFernet` support with a `UPI_ENCRYPTION_KEY_PREVIOUS` setting so rotation is now survivable.

**Low —** replaced staff photos, expense receipts, and superseded payment QRs now delete their old B2 objects (they orphaned forever); and the phone-uniqueness index that existed only in production's migration is now on the `User` model too, so tests enforce what production enforces (this immediately exposed hardcoded test phones that could collide — fixed those as well).

## Verified clean (checked, genuinely fine)

No local-disk writes anywhere in production paths (invoice PDFs render in-memory on demand); all 8 upload features go through B2 with hotel-scoped keys; the only 3 endpoints accepting raw object keys all validate prefix + DB ownership (no cross-tenant reads); migrations are a single linear head and match the models (verified via a scratch-database migrate + autogenerate diff); refresh tokens are hashed DB rows; all background sweeps use durable DB dedup columns and catch up after downtime.

## Known and accepted (documented in the memory bank, not silently ignored)

Attendance selfies uploaded but abandoned before check-in have no tracking row, so they orphan in B2 (cost only — needs a small sweep as future work); the checkout/arrival *reminder* notifications are skipped if the service sleeps through their whole window (the overdue alert still catches up); the login rate limiter resets on restart (in-process by design, documented).

**Verification:** 240/240 backend tests, ruff clean, `tsc` clean, production `next build` green. Everything is still uncommitted alongside the draft-photo feature — ready to commit and push whenever you say.

---

## What was wrong + what is now fixed

### 1. "Restore stays still" — the core complaint

After tapping **Restore**, the form filled correctly but the gold draft panel remained open at the top of the page, blocking the filled form. The user couldn't see that anything had happened.

**Fixed:** `restoreDraft()` now:

1. Sets `draftsCollapsed = true` → the draft panel immediately shrinks to a single compact header row
2. After a tiny 80 ms delay (lets the panel collapse first), **smooth-scrolls** to the Booking Details section so the filled dates/times/guest are immediately visible

### 2. "Tap on draft should do something" — the whole row should be tappable

Only the small "Restore" button was interactive. Tapping anywhere else on a draft row did nothing.

**Fixed:** The entire draft row is now a keyboard/pointer interactive element (`role="button"`). Clicking/tapping anywhere on the row triggers restore, the same as the Restore button.

### 3. Draft panel redesign — collapsible + smarter states

The gold box was always fully open with no way to minimize it even after you'd already restored a draft.

**Fixed:**

- **Collapsible header** with a `ChevronDown` arrow that animates — tap to toggle open/close
- While restoring: the row **pulses** (`animate-pulse`) so the user sees activity
- After restore: the restored draft row turns **green with a `BadgeCheck` icon** and the button reads "Restored ✓" — clear confirmation that the form is filled
- Other drafts remain discardable without triggering a new restore

### 4. Discard last draft → scroll to clean form

When discarding the last/only draft, the page now also collapses the panel and scrolls to Booking Details so the user immediately sees the clean, ready form.

### 5. "New Check-in" after success → scroll back to form

After check-in completes and the user taps "New Check-in", the walk-in form remounts (already was happening) **and** the page smooth-scrolls back to the "Walk-in Check-in" heading so the blank form is immediately visible instead of the user having to scroll up manually.
